- **agent-report**: The agent→orchestrator handoff protocol. A QA inspector is mandatory and holds veto power — a FAIL must be fixed and re-reviewed. Triggers when about to do heavy multi-file edits in the main thread, take on a task spanning many files/modules, or say "I'll just do this myself". The prime goal is to keep the main context lean and avoid overflow. Agent Team 协作:非平凡任务派子 agent,主线程只做规划/验收/冒烟测试/沟通。监理(QA)必选且有否决权。触发:将做 3+ 文件编辑/跨多模块任务,或想"我自己干完就好"时。首要目标是精简主上下文。 ([yli769227-jpg/agent-discipline](https://github.com/yli769227-jpg/agent-discipline/tree/HEAD/skills/agent-team) · ★1)
- **agent-team**: Configures and invokes the Claude Code subagent team for implementation tasks. Use this skill whenever a task involves more than one file, any physics or ML logic, or any implementation that warrants specialist review. Triggers on: "implement", "write the code for", "build", any task moved to In Progress on the board, or whenever the implementer produces code touching physics equations, numerical methods, ML architectures, or training loops. Always invoke this skill for non-trivial work — do not let the main agent implement and review its own code. ([0jg/skills](https://github.com/0jg/skills/tree/HEAD/agent-team))
- **agent-team**: Run the session as a tiered team using the full model range. ALWAYS starts with the availability interview — Fable's availability is an operator budget fact, so the skill first asks whether Fable is released, scarce, or on limits, then routes the judgment seat accordingly AND offers the deployment shape in the same interview (default seat is a second, independent Opus instance; no answer = Opus). The team is named for its shape, not a tier — the strongest released agent takes the judgment seat. Use when the user invokes /agent-team (formerly /fable-team), says "bring in Fable", "agent team",… ([akrumm92/riskmanagement](https://github.com/akrumm92/riskmanagement/tree/HEAD/scripts/assets/global-skills/agent-team))
